Use when the user runs /design-sync or says \"sync my design system to Claude Design\".","argumentHint":"[<project hint, e.g. \"Acme DS\">]","builtin":true},{"name":"dataviz","description":"Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. When the destination is a first-party document connector (host-designated, never self-described) that renders live charts, hand it the rows (inline, or as an uploaded data file the chart cites) rather than a rendered PNG/SVG — a picture of a chart loses hover, data inspection and per-value comments. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"update-config","description":"Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"verify","description":"Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"debug","description":"Enable debug logging for this session and help diagnose issues","argumentHint":"[issue description]","builtin":true},{"name":"code-review","description":"Review the current diff, or a PR number/branch/path target, for correctness bugs (plus reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups where the model's review recipe covers them) at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud); with no level given, it reuses the level you typed last. Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review. For ultra on a GitHub.com PR target, --post asks to post the finished review’s findings to the PR as a single comment from the user’s GitHub account (not a review; the launch dialog still confirms in interactive sessions, while non-interactive mode posts on the flag alone) and --no-post hides that option.","argumentHint":"[low|medium|high|xhigh|max|ultra] [--fix] [--comment] [<pr#>|<branch>|<path>]","aliases":["review"],"builtin":true},{"name":"simplify","description":"Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.","argumentHint":"[<target>]","builtin":true},{"name":"batch","description":"Research and plan a large-scale change, then execute it in parallel across 5–30 isolated worktree agents that each open a PR.","argumentHint":"<instruction>","builtin":true},{"name":"fewer-permission-prompts","description":"Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"doctor","description":"Health-check the user's Claude Code setup and fix issues: diagnose installation health — what the `claude doctor` terminal diagnostics cover — from local data (duplicate or leftover installs, PATH, unparseable settings files, broken or colliding agent definitions, skills whose frontmatter fails to parse); find unused skills, MCP servers, and plugins versus their context cost and disable dead weight; deduplicate local CLAUDE.md files against checked-in ones; trim checked-in CLAUDE.md files by cutting content a session could derive from the codebase (directory layouts, tech-stack lists, architecture overviews) while keeping gotchas, rationale, and non-standard conventions; migrate always-loaded CLAUDE.md guidance into lazy skills and nested CLAUDE.md files; flag slow hooks and context-heavy extensions; check the installed version is current; make auto mode the default permission mode; and pre-approve frequently denied read-only commands. Use when the user asks for a doctor run, checkup, audit, tune-up, or cleanup of their Claude Code setup or configuration.","argumentHint":"","aliases":["checkup"],"builtin":true},{"name":"loop","description":"Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). and mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser tool instead. It covers the pane's persistent sign-ins, tabs and preview_start, reading pages as text, site approvals, what the pane cannot open, and what to do when it cannot be reached. It is not for Claude in Chrome (mcp__claude-in-chrome__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["built-in-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:chrome-browser","description":"Read this skill before the first step that uses Claude in Chrome, the browser extension whose tools are named mcp__claude-in-chrome__* (also called Chrome, the browser extension, or the external browser) and which acts in the person's real Chrome with their own sign-ins; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__claude-in-chrome tool instead. It covers loading the tools in one ToolSearch call, checking the person's open tabs and working in a new tab, site permissions, GIF recordings, console logs, dialogs to avoid, and when to stop and ask. It is not for the built-in browser (mcp__Claude_Browser__* or mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["chrome-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:computer-use","description":"Read this skill before the first step of any request to do something in an app on the person's own computer (Notes, Finder, System Settings, any desktop app), to look at their screen, or for \"computer use\". Computer use (desktop control) lets Claude take screenshots of the person's desktop and control it with clicks, typing and scrolling through the Claude desktop app; its tools are named mcp__computer-use__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__computer_* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before computer use is turned on for a conversation there may be no such tools, only an enable__mcp__remote-devices__computer tool, which turns it on. It covers turning it on, picking the right tool, the access flow, and the safety rules for tiered apps, links and financial actions. It is not for websites, which go through Claude in Chrome or the built-in browser and their own skills. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["computer-use"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:deep-research","description":"Use this skill when the user's prompt requires (1) researching a topic across multiple sources, comparing options or alternatives, analyzing trends or history, understanding markets or industries, or reviewing literature or studies and (2) synthesizing that research into a comprehensive, narrative report. If you're planning to search the web or internal knowledge bases, consider using this skill. This skill coordinates research subagents, so use it only when you have a tool for spawning subagents (the Agent or Task tool); otherwise, research the question directly. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":""},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docs","description":"docs (living docs people share, comment on and edit; use only when the user asks for one: names a doc, document, page, memo, spec, PRD, runbook or write-up, asks for somewhere to share or keep editing something, or says yes to your doc offer; a plan, comparison, summary or notes asked in chat stays in chat (at most a one-line doc offer); a report, status update, recap or \"something I can send them\" with no form named → ask first: reply, doc or file?; tabs hold tables and live charts too; a pasted claude.ai/code/artifact/… link may be a doc: check with docs tools first; not HTML pages, apps or plain chat answers; a .docx/.pptx/.xlsx/PDF asked for by name → that format's skill): asked for one → no docs-connector instructions in context? call the docs connector's `guide` with topic.instructions first, then create the doc (headings only, no body) before any search, file read or plan, even with files attached. Documenting code means docstrings or repo docs, not a doc. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docs"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docx","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx) or Word templates (.dotx). Triggers include: any mention of 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', '.dotx', or requests to produce professional documents with formatting like tables of contents, page numbers, or letterheads. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file (to download, email or print), use this skill. However, if they ask for a document, page, report, memo, or notes WITHOUT naming a file format and the session offers Claude's own dedicated document or page skill or connector, use that instead. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or coding unrelated to document generation. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:import-memory","description":"Import a memory export from another AI assistant into Claude's memory — conversationally, additively, and with the content treated as data. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["import-memory"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:morning","description":"Render the user's morning brief as a styled HTML artifact, or set it up as a recurring weekday task. Use only when the user explicitly asks to run, see, or set up their morning brief, or if they invoke /morning by name. A question about their day, schedule, or calendar is not by itself a request for the brief; answer it directly instead. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["morning"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pdf","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pdf"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pptx","description":"Use this skill any time a .pptx or .potx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations as PowerPoint (.pptx) files; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx or .potx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email, summary, or creating a different type of slide deck); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates (.potx), layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user asks for a PowerPoint or .pptx file, or references a .pptx or .potx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. However, when the user asks for a deck, slides, a slide deck, or a presentation without naming a file format, default to using a dedicated slide-deck artifact type or a separate slides skill if this session offers one; otherwise, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pptx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:setup-writing-style","description":"Learns how the user writes from their own sent messages and docs, and builds a voice profile so future drafts sound like them instead of generic AI. The profile is saved as the my-writing-style skill. Use when the user asks to set up, learn, or capture their writing voice, or complains that drafts sound generic or unlike them and no my-writing-style profile exists. Only for drafting text the user will send as themselves, not for Claude's own replies. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["setup-writing-style"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:skill-creator","description":"Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["skill-creator"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:xlsx","description":"Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .xltx, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["xlsx"]}],"uuid":"36acc17f-27b7-4ac3-b12d-708c560cf98f","session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LgzoByQ8ohYHSUJdZK2yBB","type":"tool_result","content":"580\t  initiateCustomDomainVerification and updateBranding type their @Body() as\n581\t  CreateTenantConfigurationDto/UpdateTenantConfigurationDto/CreateApiKeyDto/CreateWebhookDto/VerifyDomainDto/UpdateBrandingDto\n582\t  — all plain `export interface`s in tenant-configuration.service.ts. Interfaces erase at compile\n583\t  time, so the param metatype is Object and the global ValidationPipe's shouldValidate returns\n584\t  false: these bodies are completely unvalidated (no whitelist, no type checks). Systemic class:\n585\t  unvalidated interface-DTO. Also FE createTenantApiKey omits createdBy that the CreateApiKeyDto\n586\t  interface requires — invisible because nothing validates it (and createdBy should come from the\n587\t  JWT, not the body). Moot while endpoints 410, latent if restored.\n588\t- **Fix design:** Convert these six interfaces into class-validator DTO classes living in\n589\t  settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts (their correct home), decorate each field, and import\n590\t  them in the controller so validation becomes automatic (tier 1). Source createApiKey's createdBy\n591\t  from the JWT via getAuthUserId (matching the C6 pattern already applied to the PUT handlers)\n592\t  rather than the request body, and drop createdBy from the FE payload. Make the whole class\n593\t  detectable: add an architecture spec that reflects over every admin-api @Body() param and asserts\n594\t  its metatype is a class carrying class-validator metadata (never an interface/Object), so an\n595\t  interface-as-DTO regression fails CI (tier 3).\n596\t- **Files to change:**\n597\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n598\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts`\n599\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts`\n600\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n601\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/body-dto-is-class.architecture.spec.ts`\n602\t- **Effort:** M\n603\t\n604\t### APA-040 [LOW] testWebhook FE method targets a route that does not exist\n605\t\n606\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n607\t- **Symptom:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs to /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test; the\n608\t  controller has no such route (would 404). The FE file itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'. Not\n609\t  reachable from this page's UI, but exported and re-exported via settingsApi.\n610\t- **Evidence:**\n611\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:323-328`\n612\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:214-243`\n613\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts:57`\n614\t- **Root cause:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test, but\n615\t  TenantConfigurationController exposes no such route (GET/POST webhooks, PUT/DELETE\n616\t  webhooks/:webhookId only) — the call 404s. The FE comment itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'.\n617\t  It is unused by TenantConfigurationPage but exported and re-exported through\n618\t  settingsApi.testWebhook. Systemic class: FE API method with no backend route\n619\t  (FE-route-with-no-backend)."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f","uuid":"235ee46b-854c-4032-a8d9-5cdc49522353","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:52:03.173Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-609425ff1373/docs/reviews/claude/2026-07-20-admin-panel-e2e-audit/findings/tenant-config.md","content":"  initiateCustomDomainVerification and updateBranding type their @Body() as\n  CreateTenantConfigurationDto/UpdateTenantConfigurationDto/CreateApiKeyDto/CreateWebhookDto/VerifyDomainDto/UpdateBrandingDto\n  — all plain `export interface`s in tenant-configuration.service.ts. Interfaces erase at compile\n  time, so the param metatype is Object and the global ValidationPipe's shouldValidate returns\n  false: these bodies are completely unvalidated (no whitelist, no type checks). Systemic class:\n  unvalidated interface-DTO. Also FE createTenantApiKey omits createdBy that the CreateApiKeyDto\n  interface requires — invisible because nothing validates it (and createdBy should come from the\n  JWT, not the body). Moot while endpoints 410, latent if restored.\n- **Fix design:** Convert these six interfaces into class-validator DTO classes living in\n  settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts (their correct home), decorate each field, and import\n  them in the controller so validation becomes automatic (tier 1). Source createApiKey's createdBy\n  from the JWT via getAuthUserId (matching the C6 pattern already applied to the PUT handlers)\n  rather than the request body, and drop createdBy from the FE payload. Make the whole class\n  detectable: add an architecture spec that reflects over every admin-api @Body() param and asserts\n  its metatype is a class carrying class-validator metadata (never an interface/Object), so an\n  interface-as-DTO regression fails CI (tier 3).\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/body-dto-is-class.architecture.spec.ts`\n- **Effort:** M\n\n### APA-040 [LOW] testWebhook FE method targets a route that does not exist\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs to /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test; the\n  controller has no such route (would 404). The FE file itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'. Not\n  reachable from this page's UI, but exported and re-exported via settingsApi.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:323-328`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:214-243`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts:57`\n- **Root cause:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test, but\n  TenantConfigurationController exposes no such route (GET/POST webhooks, PUT/DELETE\n  webhooks/:webhookId only) — the call 404s. The FE comment itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'.\n  It is unused by TenantConfigurationPage but exported and re-exported through\n  settingsApi.testWebhook. Systemic class: FE API method with no backend route\n  (FE-route-with-no-backend).","numLines":40,"startLine":580,"totalLines":1425}}}

BACKEND: delete\n232\t  TenantConfigurationController entirely (all /settings/tenant routes 404; the 410-for-writes\n233\t  convention existed to signal in-flight clients, but the sole read client is the orphaned FE page\n234\t  removed in the same commit, so keeping dead routes preserves dead surface); remove it from\n235\t  settings/controllers/index.ts and settings.module.ts. Reduce TenantConfigurationService to the\n236\t  single live method requestDefaultConfigurationProvisioning (consumed by\n237\t  tenant-provisioning.service.ts:791) — delete defaultConfiguration(), every fabricating read,\n238\t  checkStorageLimit, getConfigurationSummary, and the now-unreachable 410 stubs. Delete\n239\t  createDefaultTenantConfiguration() from settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts (nothing\n240\t  may synthesize a TenantConfiguration once the fabricator is gone) and the TenantConfiguration\n241\t  interface; keep only the section-shape interfaces (UserLimitsConfig etc.) still referenced by the\n242\t  provisioning-request DTO. Delete settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts (only the deleted\n243\t  controller used it). FRONTEND: delete TenantConfigurationPage.tsx, its lazy import + route in\n244\t  Module.tsx:30/114, services/api/tenant-config.ts, the tenantConfigApi re-export in\n245\t  services/adminApi.ts, and the TenantConfiguration interface in services/types/settings.ts. No\n246\t  capability is lost: the truthful surfaces already exist — TenantDetailPage (real\n247\t  limits/storage/modules via TenantDetailService reading auth.tenants + per-tenant schemas +\n248\t  auth.tenant_modules) and ModulesPage (real module toggling via ModuleAssignmentService → NATS\n249\t  ASSIGN_TENANT_MODULES to auth-service). The capabilities the fake page merely promised (per-tenant\n250\t  MFA policy, IP whitelists, tenant API keys, webhooks) have NO enforcement implementation anywhere\n251\t  in the platform; per the discipline they must not have UI until the feature exists — open a\n252\t  tracked product-gap finding (owner: admin-panel/product; SSoT for any future per-tenant security\n253\t  policy = auth-service enforcement + config-service storage under its own ADR) rather than\n254\t  rebuilding a fiction. Tier 3 (pattern-level, make the class detectable) — the systemic gap is that\n255\t  the existence-only contract gate cannot see fabricated reads. Add\n256\t  apps/admin-api-service/src/**tests**/retired-endpoint-symmetry.spec.ts to the same dedicated\n257\t  admin-route-contract CI project: (1) a RETIRED_ROUTE_PREFIXES SSoT (['settings/tenant'], each\n258\t  entry citing its drop migration + replacement surface) — no @Controller/@Get route may resolve\n259\t  under a retired prefix, so the surface cannot silently reappear without a real store; (2)\n260\t  retirement-symmetry rule: for every service file referencing GoneException (a retired-store\n261\t  adapter), every public method must either throw Gone or appear in the spec's documented per-class\n262\t  allowlist (here exactly requestDefaultConfigurationProvisioning) — a non-throwing read on a\n263\t  retired adapter (the fabrication shape) fails CI repo-wide within admin-api; (3) zero references\n264\t  to createDefault\\* factories from non-test, non-provisioning code. Same-commit bookkeeping (these\n265\t  gates are designed to break here): contract-validation.spec.ts — drop the\n266\t  '/settings/tenant/:param/webhooks/:param/test' KNOWN_EXCEPTIONS entry and update the backend\n267\t  endpoint snapshot (603 minus the deleted tenant-config routes); trim the\n268\t  TenantConfigurationService mock in tenant-provisioning.service.spec.ts to the kept surface.\n269\t- **Files to change:**\n270\t  -      raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n390\t    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n391\t    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n392\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n393\t    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n394\t    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n395\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n396\t    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n397\t    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n398\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n399\t    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n400\t    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n401\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n402\t\n403\t    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n404\t    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n405\t        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n406\t    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n407\t    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n408\t    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n409\t        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n410\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n411\t        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n412\t        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n413\t        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n414\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n415\t        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n416\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n417\t        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n418\t        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n419\t            raise GovernanceError(\n420\t                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n421\t            )\n422\t        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n423\t            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n424\t            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n425\t                raise GovernanceError(\n426\t                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n427\t                )\n428\t            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n429\t            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n430\t            _ensure_string_list(\n431\t                evidence_refs,\n432\t                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n433\t                allow_empty=False,\n434\t            )\n435\t        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n436\t\n437\t    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n438\t    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n439\t        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n440\t\n441\t    # Plan claim_id or\n444\t    # agent_id differs. Pre-fix a stitched envelope from a different\n445\t    # lease (same request_id but different claim/agent) passed because\n446\t    # only request_id was cross-checked.\n447\t    if lease is not None:\n448\t        leased_claim = lease.get(\"claim_id\")\n449\t        leased_agent = lease.get(\"agent_id\")\n450\t        if leased_claim is not None and envelope[\"claim_id\"] != leased_claim:\n451\t            raise GovernanceError(\n452\t                f\"envelope_claim_id_mismatch: envelope.claim_id=\"\n453\t                f\"{envelope['claim_id']!r} does not match leased claim_id=\"\n454\t                f\"{leased_claim!r}\"\n455\t            )\n456\t        if leased_agent is not None and envelope[\"agent_id\"] != leased_agent:\n457\t            raise GovernanceError(\n458\t                f\"envelope_agent_id_mismatch: envelope.agent_id=\"\n459\t                f\"{envelope['agent_id']!r} does not match leased agent_id=\"\n460\t                f\"{leased_agent!r}\"\n461\t            )\n462\t\n463\t    if request is not None:\n464\t        if envelope[\"request_id\"] != request.get(\"request_id\"):\n465\t            raise GovernanceError(\n466\t                f\"agent-response.request_id {envelope['request_id']!r} does not match request \"\n467\t                f\"{request.get('request_id')!r}\"\n468\t            )\n469\t        # Plan 024 v3 §H-4 — envelope.role must match request.role.\n470\t        # Pre-fix only the membership check above (line 322) ensured\n471\t        # the role was in REQUEST_ROLES; a request bound to role\n472\t        # `domain_review` could accept a response with role\n473\t        # `architectural_judgment` and pass cross-checks because the\n474\t        # equality with request.role was never asserted. The\n475\t        # downstream judgment_bridge + consensus path reads\n476\t        # response.role to route the verdict, so a mismatched role\n477\t        # silently misroutes the judgment.\n478\t        request_role = request.get(\"role\")\n479\t        if request_role is not None and envelope.get(\"role\") != request_role:\n480\t            raise GovernanceError(\n481\t                f\"response_role_mismatch_with_request: \"\n482\t                f\"envelope.role={envelope.get('role')!r}, \""}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f","uuid":"b887274d-2a3f-44e4-8845-20fd811f99f5","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:52:56.862Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-609425ff1373/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_contract.py","content":"def validate_response(\n    envelope: dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    lease: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Validate an `aria/agent-response/v1` envelope.\n\n    `request` is optional — when provided, the response is cross-checked\n    against the request's `must_satisfy` ids and `expected_output_path`.\n\n    `lease` is optional — when provided (Plan 023 v3 §A-5), the\n    envelope's claim_id and agent_id MUST match the leased identity.\n    Pre-Plan-023 the envelope's claim_id was only validated for SHAPE\n    (CLAIM_ID_RE pattern) and the request_id was matched to the\n    request, but envelope.claim_id and envelope.agent_id were never\n    bound to the lease that authorized the submission. A stitched\n    envelope from a different lease (same request_id but different\n    claim/agent) would pass.\n    \"\"\"\n    missing = [f for f in RESPONSE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if f not in envelope]\n    if missing:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n\n    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n            )\n        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n                )\n            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n            _ensure_string_list(\n                evidence_refs,\n                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n                allow_empty=False,\n            )\n        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n\n    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n\n    # Plan 023 v3 §A-5 — envelope claim/agent identity bound to lease.\n    # When the caller supplies the leased claim+agent identity (which\n    # submit_claim_result does), reject any envelope whose claim_id or\n    # agent_id differs. Pre-fix a stitched envelope from a different\n    # lease (same request_id but different claim/agent) passed because\n    # only request_id was cross-checked.\n    if lease is not None:\n        leased_claim = lease.get(\"claim_id\")\n        leased_agent = lease.get(\"agent_id\")\n        if leased_claim is not None and envelope[\"claim_id\"] != leased_claim:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_claim_id_mismatch: envelope.claim_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['claim_id']!r} does not match leased claim_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_claim!r}\"\n            )\n        if leased_agent is not None and envelope[\"agent_id\"] != leased_agent:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_agent_id_mismatch: envelope.agent_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['agent_id']!r} does not match leased agent_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_agent!r}\"\n            )\n\n    if request is not None:\n        if envelope[\"request_id\"] != request.get(\"request_id\"):\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"agent-response.request_id {envelope['request_id']!r} does not match request \"\n                f\"{request.get('request_id')!r}\"\n            )\n        # Plan 024 v3 §H-4 — envelope.role must match request.role.\n        # Pre-fix only the membership check above (line 322) ensured\n        # the role was in REQUEST_ROLES; a request bound to role\n        # `domain_review` could accept a response with role\n        # `architectural_judgment` and pass cross-checks because the\n        # equality with request.role was never asserted. The\n        # downstream judgment_bridge + consensus path reads\n        # response.role to route the verdict, so a mismatched role\n        # silently misroutes the judgment.\n        request_role = request.get(\"role\")\n        if request_role is not None and envelope.get(\"role\") != request_role:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"response_role_mismatch_with_request: \"\n                f\"envelope.role={envelope.get('role')!r}, \"","numLines":120,"startLine":363,"totalLines":616}}}

ts-node\n// D5-documentation dimension adapter v1 (Plan \"ARIA Sinir Sistemi\" FAZ 7).\n//\n// WHY: nothing watched documentation truth — a runbook can name a script\n// that was deleted a year ago and stay green forever. Stale docs are worse\n// than missing docs: they answer confidently and wrongly.\n// WHAT (deterministic, fs-only): every backtick-quoted repo path in\n// docs/**/*.md is resolved against the working tree; a reference to a path\n// that no longer exists is a `doc_references_missing_path` finding with the\n// doc's file:line as evidence. Globs, placeholders, and line-suffixed refs\n// are handled so the rule stays low-noise.\nimport { relative } from 'node:path';\n\nimport {\n  collectFiles,\n  filterFilesBySnapshot,\n  normalizeWorkspacePath,\n  readWorkspaceFile,\n  requireScanRoots,\n  resolveInsideWorkspace,\n  workspacePathExists,\n} from './adapter-fs';\n\ninterface AdapterInput {\n  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n  readonly repo_snapshot?: { readonly allowed_paths?: readonly string[] };\n}\n\ninterface EvidenceRef {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterObservation {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly type: string;\n  readonly path?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterFinding {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly rule: 'doc_references_missing_path';\n  readonly severity: 'medium';\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line: number;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly evidence: readonly EvidenceRef[];\n  readonly confidence?: number;\n}\n\ninterface AriaOutput {\n  readonly observations: readonly AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: readonly AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly read_paths: readonly string[];\n  readonly evidence_sources: readonly string[];\n  readonly belief_candidates: readonly unknown[];\n  readonly cost_units: number;\n  readonly metadata: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n// A ref must start at a known top-level code directory to count as a repo\n// path claim — prose like `feature/branch-name` or `owner/repo` never\n// matches, which is what keeps this rule quiet.\nconst PATH_PREFIXES = [\n  'apps/',\n  'e2e/',\n  'infrastructure/',\n  'libs/',\n  'platform/',\n  'scripts/',\n  'sens-api-gateway/',\n  'tools/',\n  'web/',\n];\n\nconst BACKTICK_SPAN_RE = /`([^`\\n]+)`/g;\n\nfunction candidateRef(span: string): string | undefined {\n  let text = span.trim();\n  // `path:123` / `path:12-40` — evidence-style refs pin a line, the claim\n  // is about the path.\n  text = text.replace(/:[0-9][0-9-]*$/, '');\n  if (!PATH_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => text.startsWith(prefix))) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  // Globs and placeholders are patterns, not path claims.\n  if (/[*{}<>$\\s]/.test(text) || text.includes('...')) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_@./-]+$/.test(text)) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  return text.replace(/\\/$/, '');\n}\n\nexport function analyzeDocStaleness(\n  input: AdapterInput,\n  workspaceRoot = process.cwd(),\n): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('doc-staleness-adapter', input.roots);\n  const observations: AdapterObservation[] = [];\n  const findings: AdapterFinding[] = [];\n  const readPaths: string[] = [];\n\n  const docs = filterFilesBySnapshot(\n    roots\n      .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n      .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n      .flatMap((root) => collectFiles(root, { extensions: ['.md'] })),\n    workspaceRoot,\n    input,\n  );\n\n  let refsChecked = 0;\n  for (const doc of docs) {\n    const docRel = normalizeWorkspacePath(relative(workspaceRoot, doc));\n    readPaths.push(docRel);\n    const lines = readWorkspaceFile(doc).split('\\n');\n    let missingInDoc = 0;\n    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {\n      for (const match of lines[index].matchAll(BACKTICK_SPAN_RE)) {\n        const ref = candidateRef(match[1]);\n        if (ref === undefined) {\n          continue;\n        }\n        refsChecked += 1;\n        if (workspacePathExists(resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, ref))) {\n          continue;\n        }\n        missingInDoc += 1;\n        findings.push({\n          id: `doc-staleness:missing:${docRel}:${index + 1}:${ref}`,\n          rule: 'doc_references_missing_path',\n          severity: 'medium',\n          path: docRel,\n          line: index + 1,\n          message:\n            `\\`${docRel}\\` references \\`${ref}\\`, which no longer exists — ` +\n            'the doc answers confidently about a surface that is gone.',\n          evidence: [{ path: docRel, line: index + 1 }],\n          confidence: 0.85,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n    observations.push({\n      id: `doc-staleness:doc:${docRel}`,\n      type: 'doc_staleness_document',\n      path: docRel,\n      details: { missingRefs: missingInDoc },\n    });\n  }\n\n  const sortedReadPaths = [...new Set(readPaths)].sort();\n  return {\n    observations: observations.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),\n    findings: findings.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id)),\n    read_paths: sortedReadPaths,\n    evidence_sources: sortedReadPaths,\n    belief_candidates: {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016eNsKSiGgJrTt9JmpQzfTV","type":"tool_result","content":"548\t  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist as @IsString({each}) — arbitrary strings, CIDR included. So the same\n549\t  domain concept ('allowed/blocked address or range') has two contradictory validators, and the UI\n550\t  invites input that only the bulk path accepts. A CIDR entry via Add 400s (@IsIP) while a bare IP\n551\t  410s — two failure modes for one button.\n552\t- **Fix design:** Encode the real domain rule once as a custom class-validator constraint\n553\t  @IsIpOrCidr() and apply it to BOTH IpAddressDto.ip and the each-item validation of\n554\t  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist in UpdateTenantSecurityDto, replacing the mismatched @IsIP/@IsString pair\n555\t  (tier 1: the type system now expresses exactly what the UI offers, consistently across single-add\n556\t  and bulk). Add a unit spec covering bare IPv4, IPv6, valid CIDR (accepted) and garbage (rejected).\n557\t  If downstream enforcement genuinely cannot honor CIDR, the alternative root-cause fix is to strip\n558\t  'ranges' from the copy and set a bare-IP placeholder — but the 'ranges' intent plus the\n559\t  CIDR-accepting bulk path indicate CIDR should be supported.\n560\t- **Files to change:**\n561\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n562\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/validators/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.ts`\n563\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/__tests__/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.spec.ts`\n564\t- **Effort:** S\n565\t\n566\t### APA-039 [LOW] Unvalidated bodies on several endpoints: controller uses service interfaces as DTOs\n567\t\n568\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n569\t- **Symptom:** createApiKey, createWebhook, domain verify, and updateBranding take types imported\n570\t  from the service file (plain TS interfaces, not class-validator classes), so the global\n571\t  ValidationPipe skips them entirely (non-class metatype). Additionally the FE createTenantApiKey\n572\t  payload omits 'createdBy' which the CreateApiKeyDto interface requires — invisible at runtime\n573\t  because nothing validates it. Moot while the endpoints 410, but a latent validation gap if\n574\t  restored.\n575\t- **Evidence:**\n576\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:18-26`\n577\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:46-51`\n578\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:263-271`\n579\t- **Root cause:** createConfiguration, updateConfiguration, createApiKey, createWebhook,\n580\t  initiateCustomDomainVerification and updateBranding type their @Body() as\n581\t  CreateTenantConfigurationDto/UpdateTenantConfigurationDto/CreateApiKeyDto/CreateWebhookDto/VerifyDomainDto/UpdateBrandingDto"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f","uuid":"15877af3-a59c-4b9a-a70a-16e0b98ce673","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:53:31.282Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-609425ff1373/docs/reviews/claude/2026-07-20-admin-panel-e2e-audit/findings/tenant-config.md","content":"  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist as @IsString({each}) — arbitrary strings, CIDR included. So the same\n  domain concept ('allowed/blocked address or range') has two contradictory validators, and the UI\n  invites input that only the bulk path accepts. A CIDR entry via Add 400s (@IsIP) while a bare IP\n  410s — two failure modes for one button.\n- **Fix design:** Encode the real domain rule once as a custom class-validator constraint\n  @IsIpOrCidr() and apply it to BOTH IpAddressDto.ip and the each-item validation of\n  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist in UpdateTenantSecurityDto, replacing the mismatched @IsIP/@IsString pair\n  (tier 1: the type system now expresses exactly what the UI offers, consistently across single-add\n  and bulk). Add a unit spec covering bare IPv4, IPv6, valid CIDR (accepted) and garbage (rejected).\n  If downstream enforcement genuinely cannot honor CIDR, the alternative root-cause fix is to strip\n  'ranges' from the copy and set a bare-IP placeholder — but the 'ranges' intent plus the\n  CIDR-accepting bulk path indicate CIDR should be supported.\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/validators/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/__tests__/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.spec.ts`\n- **Effort:** S\n\n### APA-039 [LOW] Unvalidated bodies on several endpoints: controller uses service interfaces as DTOs\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** createApiKey, createWebhook, domain verify, and updateBranding take types imported\n  from the service file (plain TS interfaces, not class-validator classes), so the global\n  ValidationPipe skips them entirely (non-class metatype). Additionally the FE createTenantApiKey\n  payload omits 'createdBy' which the CreateApiKeyDto interface requires — invisible at runtime\n  because nothing validates it. Moot while the endpoints 410, but a latent validation gap if\n  restored.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:18-26`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:46-51`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:263-271`\n- **Root cause:** createConfiguration, updateConfiguration, createApiKey, createWebhook,\n  initiateCustomDomainVerification and updateBranding type their @Body() as\n  CreateTenantConfigurationDto/UpdateTenantConfigurationDto/CreateApiKeyDto/CreateWebhookDto/VerifyDomainDto/UpdateBrandingDto","numLines":34,"startLine":548,"totalLines":1425}}}

Removal completes\nthe retirement decision already made: unmount the route from Module.tsx; delete\npages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx, services/api/tenant-config.ts, the tenantConfigApi\nre-exports/delegations in services/api/settings.ts and services/adminApi.ts, and the\nTenantConfiguration type block in services/types/settings.ts; delete the backend tombstone adapter\n(settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts,\nsettings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts, settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts,\nsettings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts) and its settings.module.ts wiring. Also remove\ntenant-provisioning.service.ts's call to requestDefaultConfigurationProvisioning — it fabricates a\n'REQUESTED' record that no transport ever delivers, and config-service's hierarchical\neffective-configuration already makes SYSTEM-row fallback automatic for new tenants (tier 2: correct\ndefault with zero per-tenant seeding). If product later wants a per-tenant admin configuration\nsurface, that is a new feature that must start from a config-service contract change (admin-only\ntenantId override arg, fail-closed) and gets its own tracked finding — this commit ships no partial\nsurface.\n\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/contract-validation.spec.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/Module.tsx`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/adminApi.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/types/settings.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/ProvisioningSettingsPage.tsx`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/platform-configuration.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/__tests__/platform-configuration.spec.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/settings.module.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/tenant/services/tenant-provisioning.service.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/system-management/services/global-settings.service.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/system-management/controllers/global-settings.controller.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/system-management/__tests__/provisioning-config.spec.ts`\n  - `apps/config-service/src/database/migrations/1805500000000-SeedProvisioningConfigurations.ts`","numLines":40,"startLine":1336,"totalLines":1425}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y8DTgwLgWuHkuvdZJjaoYj","type":"tool_result","content":"455\t    if digest != recorded:\n456\t        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n457\t    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n458\t\n459\t\n460\tdef _check_agent_ref(\n461\t    ref: str,\n462\t    *,\n463\t    root: Path,\n464\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n465\t    checked: list[str],\n466\t    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n467\t    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n468\t) -> None:\n469\t    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n470\t    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n471\t    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n472\t    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n473\t    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n474\t    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n475\t    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n476\t    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n477\t    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n478\t    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n479\t    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n480\t    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n481\t        checked.append(ref)\n482\t        return\n483\t    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n484\t        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n485\t        if admissible:\n486\t            checked.append(ref)\n487\t            return\n488\t        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n489\t            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n490\t            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n491\t            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n492\t            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n493\t            errors.append({\n494\t                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n495\t                \"ref\": ref,\n496\t                \"reason\": reason,\n497\t            })\n498\t            return\n499\t    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n500\t    if parsed is None:\n501\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n502\t        return\n503\t    path, line = parsed\n504\t    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n505\t    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n506\t    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n507\t    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n508\t    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n509\t    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n510\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n511\t    try:\n512\t        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n513\t    except _GE as exc:\n514\t        msg = str(exc)\n515\t        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n516\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n517\t        else:\n518\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n519\t                           \"error\": msg})\n520\t        return\n521\t    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n522\t    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n523\t    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n524\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n525\t        return\n526\t    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n527\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n528\t        return\n529\t    checked.append(rel_str)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f","uuid":"02f61cf7-5e18-4a80-b298-f17d6893685c","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:53:59.674Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-609425ff1373/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"    if digest != recorded:\n        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n\n\ndef _check_agent_ref(\n    ref: str,\n    *,\n    root: Path,\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checked: list[str],\n    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n        checked.append(ref)\n        return\n    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n        if admissible:\n            checked.append(ref)\n            return\n        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n            })\n            return\n    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n    if parsed is None:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n        return\n    path, line = parsed\n    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n    try:\n        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n    except _GE as exc:\n        msg = str(exc)\n        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n        else:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n                           \"error\": msg})\n        return\n    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n        return\n    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n        return\n    checked.append(rel_str)","numLines":75,"startLine":455,"totalLines":774}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MtE37T4gHpc6VXDxSh8G8L","type":"tool_result","content":"2228\t    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n2229\t    #\n2230\t    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n2231\t    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n2232\t    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n2233\t    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n2234\t    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n2235\t    # which fails:\n2236\t    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n2237\t    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n2238\t    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n2239\t    #\n2240\t    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n2241\t    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n2242\t    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n2243\t    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n2244\t    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n2245\t    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n2246\t    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n2247\t    #\n2248\t    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n2249\t    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n2250\t    # internal kernel identity fields.\n2251\t    if completed.stdout:\n2252\t        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2253\t            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2254\t            request_id=request_id,\n2255\t            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n2256\t            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n2257\t            role=role,\n2258\t            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n2259\t            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n2260\t            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n2261\t        )\n2262\t        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n2263\t        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2264\t        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n2265\t        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2266\t        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n2267\t        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n2268\t        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n2269\t        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n2270\t        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n2271\t        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n2272\t        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.\n2273\t        if (\n2274\t            completed.returncode == 0\n2275\t            and _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY is False\n2276\t            and request_envelope is not None\n2277\t            and tools_dir is not None"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f","uuid":"0f183a46-1856-4a5d-b192-298f04de5f6c","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:54:30.817Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-609425ff1373/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n    #\n    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n    # which fails:\n    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n    #\n    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n    #\n    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n    # internal kernel identity fields.\n    if completed.stdout:\n        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n            request_id=request_id,\n            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n            role=role,\n            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n        )\n        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.\n        if (\n            completed.returncode == 0\n            and _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY is False\n            and request_envelope is not None\n            and tools_dir is not None","numLines":50,"startLine":2228,"totalLines":5265}}}

D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n224\t    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n225\t    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n226\t    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n227\t    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n228\t    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n229\t    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n230\t    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n231\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n232\t    if not judge_id:\n233\t        raise GovernanceError(\n234\t            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n235\t            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n236\t        )\n237\t    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n238\t        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n239\t        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n240\t        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n241\t        raise GovernanceError(\n242\t            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n243\t        )\n244\t\n245\t    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n246\t    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n247\t    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n248\t    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n249\t\n250\t    note = (\n251\t        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n252\t        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n253\t        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n254\t        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n255\t    )\n256\t    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n257\t        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n258\t\n259\t    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n260\t    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n261\t\n262\t    row = record_operator_feedback(\n263\t        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n264\t        run_id=str(run_id),\n265\t        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n266\t        verdict=verdict,\n267\t        severity=severity,\n268\t        note=note,\n269\t        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n270\t        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n271\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n272\t        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n273\t        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n274\t        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n275\t        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n276\t        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n277\t        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n278\t        # predate the stamp.\n279\t        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n280\t        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n281\t        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n282\t        rationale=str(note),\n283\t        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n284\t        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n285\t        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n286\t        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n287\t        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n288\t        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n289\t        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n290\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n291\t        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n292\t        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n293\t        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n294\t        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n295\t        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n296\t        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n297\t        base_dir=base_dir,\n298\t    )\n299\t    return row\n300\t\n301\t\n302\tdef persist_supporting_payload(\n303\t    *,\n304\t    request: dict[str, Any],"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"51ac464f-145d-4338-b75b-0c6c41fc7f1f","uuid":"cf50b4e6-c13f-4424-a125-465d5c2d5e7a","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:54:47.371Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-609425ff1373/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n        rationale=str(note),\n        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n        base_dir=base_dir,\n    )\n    return row\n\n\ndef persist_supporting_payload(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],","numLines":105,"startLine":200,"totalLines":388}}}
